Title: Brevis notitia fundationis Theodori Koriathovits, olim ducis de Munkacs, pro religiosis Ruthenis Ordinis Sancti Basilii Magni in Monte Csernek ad Munkacs anno MCCCLX factae, exhibens seriem episcoporum Graeco-Catholicorum Munkacsiensium ... e variis diplomatibus, decretisque regiis ac aliis documentis authenticis potissimum concinnatam; Pars prima
Creator: Joannicius Basilovits, of the Order of St Basil the Great, proto-hegumen of the monastery on Monte Csernek at Munkács
Publisher: Cassoviae (Košice): ex typographia Ellingeriana, 1799
Publication Date: 1799
Rights: Public Domain (published 1799, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: lat
Source: Internet Archive item brevisnotitiafun01basi (https://archive.org/details/brevisnotitiafun01basi), the item's own brevisnotitiafun01basi_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Petro Jacyk Program - Centre for Russian and East European Studies; host metadata date: 1799. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 142a5e76b20ae530e2b5b0f85cda1c5c8ebbcbab9adfa902d059c4f76690abab.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. University of Toronto (Petro Jacyk Program) scan; clean. The book is in several parts; this is Part I. Part IV, tome II (Košice 1804) is on the host as brevisnotitiafun02basi and is recorded as a lead.
Content note: A Ruthenian Basilian superior's history of the Munkács monastery and of the Greek Catholic bishops of Munkács, built on charters, royal decrees and other documents, many quoted in full.
